Added guard (not in the request): the Y rule does not fire while a visible peer is within 40 m in our block (a cutscene that legitimately ends lower, next to the others). Not done: "the nearest peer that watched the same remo id" (the snapshot carries a bool, not the id); only P0 is used.
- Tests: `finished_truth_table`, `active_needs_playing_and_not_finished`, tracker tests (edges once, flicker, fall, near peer, block, death latch, never-saw), `pause_shifts_by_the_frame_delta_but_not_by_a_hitch`, protocol `snapshot_hidden_byte_is_bits` and the extended round trip, net `a_cutscene_snapshot_is_hidden_but_not_a_loading_screen`, `party_row_state_per_peer` (3), bossgate `waiting_flags_freeze_during_a_peer_cutscene` (a learned flag is not due during 5 min of cutscene and is due 88 s after it, 90 s in all), game `the_give_up_clock_stops_while_somebody_is_in_a_cutscene`, bossrules `cutscene_postpones_releases_and_pauses_the_wait_clocks`.

**B. NPC talk lock** (`talklock.rs`, `npcintro.rs`).
- Game side [VAR]: `TALK_MAN = base+0x3EA0CE0`. The press handler `0x140A775B0(this, float dt)` (called from `0x140A746F0` at `0x140A747E6`, every frame) ends in `*(u8)(TalkMan+0x20E0) = pressed; *(i32)(TalkMan+0x20E4) = talk id (-1 none)`. It has two exits (the tail `return sub_1411D17B0(..)` and the `LABEL_65` store), so the hook wraps the whole function.
- **Hook feasibility: yes.** The first 31 bytes are nine instructions without RIP-relative operands (`4C 8B DC 49 89 5B 20 55 57 41 54 41 56 41 57 49 8D 6B A1 48 81 EC A0 00 00 00 41 0F 29 73 C8`); `hook::install_detour` relocates them (31 <= 32). The detour calls the original through the trampoline (`rcx`, `xmm1`) and then runs the post-check: pressed != 0 and the NPC locked by another peer (or host-only) -> write `+0x20E0 = 0`, `+0x20E4 = -1`, overlay notice "<name> is talking to this NPC" (once per 2 s), log `talklock: talk press on NPC .. vetoed`. If the install fails, the same check runs in the frame task and logs `ORDERING FAILED (no hook)` when a conversation still opened on a locked NPC.
- Talk instance -> NPC [VAR + I]: enumeration `TalkMan+0x28` (count), `+0x30` (blocks, 0xB0 each), per block `+0x80` (pointer array) and `+0x88` (count), id at `+0x98`, as requested. `0x1411C9D80(a1)` does `v = *a1; blockIdx = *(i32)(v+0x20); chr = 0x140A4A010(WorldChrMan, blockIdx, *(u32)(a1+8))` (range checked), i.e. it takes a chr HANDLE, not necessarily the talk instance: the class methods at `0x1411C5110/5150/5180` call it with `*(this+8)`. Both forms are tried (the instance, then `*(instance+8)`), the first that gives a chr with an entity id > 0 wins, logged once as `talklock: NPC chr found with 0x1411C9D80(talk ins | *(talk ins + 8))`. If neither works the key is `0x40000000 | talk id` (the talk id is the same parameter on every machine).
- `EV_TALK_LOCK = 42` (free: 1-4, 6-8, 40, 41 are in use): a = key (entity id), b = 1 start / refresh every 5 s, 0 end. Sent to everybody; the session relays an event from a client through the host (`send_event` = `Dest::All`), so there is no world.rs relay code. The table holds OTHER players' locks (`Locks`): the first holder keeps a lock, only the owner clears it, 20 s without a refresh expires it, a leaving peer's locks are dropped each frame (`retain_peers`), link down clears all. Our conversation: the talk id of the last press that passed the veto, then `step_mine` follows `game::talk_idle()` with a 1.5 s end grace.
- `[GAMEPLAY] quest_choices_host_only = 0` (settings.rs template + parse -> `talklock::HOST_ONLY`): on a client a press on a quest NPC is vetoed with "Only the host can talk to quest NPCs."; the host's `TalkOrder::admit_with(.., host_only)` drops every client `EV_TALK_FLAG` (they are the group-7 flags of a conversation, i.e. quest NPC blocks). "Quest NPC" = `npcintro::quest_block_of(key)`: there is no entity -> flag block table in the data we have, so it is LEARNED: our conversation's talk-flag writes (`Intro::own_write`) and a peer's `EV_TALK_FLAG` arriving while that peer holds a lock (`talklock::note_peer_talk_flag`) record key -> block.
- Tests: lock expiry and refresh, first holder / owner-only clear, peer leave for 1-5 peers, `veto_decision` (locked by another peer, own conversation, expired, host-only on/off, quest vs ordinary NPC, host), `our_conversation_starts_refreshes_and_ends_after_the_grace`, npcintro `host_only_drops_every_clients_talk_flags` and `quest_npcs_are_learned_from_talk_flags`, settings `quest_choices_host_only_is_in_the_file_and_parses`.

**Risks.** (1) The veto relies on the consumer reading `+0x20E0/+0x20E4` after the handler in the same frame [I]; if a conversation starts from another path nothing blocks it (the fallback log shows it). (2) The chr lookup call is unverified live (both argument forms); a wrong one only yields the fallback key, but the fallback key and the entity key differ, so two machines that resolve differently do not see each other's locks. (3) `quest_choices_host_only` cannot stop the FIRST conversation with an NPC a client has never talked to (nothing learned yet); that talk's flags are dropped on the host, later talks are vetoed. (4) A cutscene bit from a VERSION 12 build is not understood: it is refused at connect, as intended. A "cutscene active" state that sticks holds warps and expulsions until it clears and spectate releases for at most 60 s. (5) The re-placement trusts the position of the first active frame; the Y rule can move a player whose cutscene legitimately ended 10+ m lower with nobody near (they land back where they began). (6) A peer in a cutscene is treated as not engaged in a boss fight for the duration (its snapshot is hidden), so a death of ours during that time can still reload us.

## 48. Wipe lives guard, no boss stealth claims, steady engage, spectate activation, log handle, frame hitches (v8.6, 2026-10-25, NOT verified live)

Live test 2026-10-25 06:00-06:16 (Genichiro 1110800 / Tomoe phase 1110801), host log ~1792901004-901788, friend `log-Friend2-20261025-061857.txt` (+8 s).

- **Two bosses after a wipe.** Friend died 901636 (host time), reloaded ~901638; the host spectated (phase B) and reloaded 901643. In between, the friend's posture took the host's old-world state: `posture: 1110800 lives 2 -> 0 (host)` (friend 901647). Its fresh Genichiro died, phase 2 and its cutscene started and placed the friend in the arena. The friend's livesync then sent lives 0 into the host's fresh world (`livesync: enemy 1110800 netId 0x1105: lives 2 -> 0 (the friend's deathblow)`, host 901658): two bosses. Fix: `persist::after_death_guard(window)` = dead and not reloaded, or < window since the load after our death. posture.rs `client_step` writes no host lives inside 20 s (`DEATH_LIVES_GUARD`); livesync.rs drops every incoming lives state inside 20 s (`DEATH_GUARD`, counted as "ignored after our death reload"; dropped rather than held because `merge` keeps the minimum). A plain (non-wipe) death still gets the boss back from persist's restore (8 s).
- **Boss jumped between players.** The friend's stealth claim took 1110800 / 1110801 "behind" in the fight, the host kept them: `claimed by both machines` from 901341 to 901362. Fix: enemies.rs `stealth_claims` skips the gauge entity and every bosses.rs encounter member.
- **Long black screens (host).** After the second wipe the host engaged while its world update had only just run (`waiting: our world update is paused` until 901747, engage 901763): `hook: freeze slow: 106 threads, 7357 ms`, then the restore 5091 ms. Threads still streaming the world are slow to stop. Fix: nsession `world_steady` = running for `ENGAGE_STEADY` 3 s without a stall (`running_since`), required where `world_running` was.
- **Spectate slideshow (cause not proven).** While we spectate, puppet.rs passes the living peers to activation.rs on any role and in the native session too (forced every-frame interval near them), since the watched fight is far from our dead player by the game's distance rule.
- **Host FPS.** Mod tick ~1 ms; 2 s fps averages hide single long frames. lib.rs `Hitches`: the `coop: tick` line adds `long frames >25 ms N >50 ms M, worst X ms (mod Y ms of it)`. log.rs keeps the file open (no create_dir + CreateFile + close per line on the game thread). fps.rs: the install line no longer says "unlocked to 120" when `fps_unlock = 0` (the `fps: limit` line is the state).

## 50. No stealth claims on an enemy another player fights; the join jump (v8.8, 2026-10-25, NOT verified live)

- **Enemies "forget" the players and give stealth deathblows.** enemies.rs `stealth_claims` (native session) claims purely by geometry: behind within `stealth_window_m` (15 m) or above. An enemy fighting the host went to the friend's AI when the friend stepped behind it; that AI knew nothing of the fight (the host's AI state and SpEffects are never sent, RE-BACKSTAB.md), so the enemy dropped its target and the stealth gate opened. Fix: no claim while another living player (snapshot hp > 0) is within `FIGHT_NEAR_M` 8 m of the enemy (`fought_by_another`, unit tested). An unaware enemy nobody stands next to is still claimed. Awareness itself (`IsBattleState` 0x85920, awareness list 0x626430) is not read: untested RE.
- **The ~260 m jump at a join** (not fixed): every native remote, right after its creation, is moved by the game 244-268 m off our pin for 1-3 s (`remote moved 268.6 m ... target 268.6 m from cur`, host and friend, every creation 2026-10-25) until its NetworkManipulator serves packets (`inbox ... served 0`). Probably the creation position (CharaInitParam / PGD slot). Needs RE of the spawn position or a draw toggle; no known visibility field, so nothing is written blind.

## 51. Fight radius 20 m, spectate lock dot (v8.10, 2026-10-25, NOT verified live)

- **`FIGHT_NEAR_M` 8 -> 20 m** (enemies.rs): v8.8 left 123 `claimed by both machines` in 18 min live; the friend still claimed enemies the host fought from a roof or from below at 10-20 m. Unit test extended (25 m counts, 40 m does not).
- **Spectate lock dot:** players could not tell whether the lock-on was active and toggled it twice. `spectate_cam::lock_mark()` publishes the locked target point (lock height added), `ui.rs draw_lock_mark` projects it with the render camera and draws a white dot in a dark ring. Cleared on unlock, restore and reset.
- Lib tests 342/342, `cargo check` ok, release build ok.

## 53. Boss owner fixed to the host, resurrection grace (v8.12, 2026-10-25, NOT verified live)

- **Boss jumps behind the player** (host and client report it, live 2026-10-25): ownfill (nsession.rs `ownfill`) gave an ownerless boss to the NEAREST player (host 5 m ahead), so Genichiro / Tomoe (1110800 / 1110801) flipped between the machines (host log 909076 taken, 909211 `given back after 3917 ms (another player owns it)`, friend 909093 taken, given back after 1024 ms; ownerless reports show the boss with both players within 4 m and no owner). A second AI ran the copy until the owner change snapped it. The log hid this because ownfill logs stop after 30 lines. Fix: `wanted_boss` / `keep_boss`: the host owns every encounter member within 150 m, a client only when the host is clearly farther; the host never drops a boss in a conflict (the client drops at once); boss ownfill lines are tagged `(boss)` and never rate limited.
- **No Resurrect although the friend had one** (headless, 910489, again): `no_revive_window` latched spectate on the very first dead frame with both windows closed. The windows may open a few frames later (the game itself reads them one frame after the dead predicate), so the dead-step detour now holds the answer `REVIVE_GRACE_MS` (2.5 s) and lets the game run its own menu when a window opens. Logs: `death: both resurrection windows closed ...` and `death: a resurrection window opened N ms after ...`, plus the fields in the spectate start line. If the friend still cannot resurrect, the fields are not the right signal (charge count lives elsewhere); then phase B must be turned off (`spectate_camera`) until it is found.

The friend said (certain) he had a Resurrect in every boss-fight death where phase B started (log-Friend2 832197, 840486, 910497, host 842950: `spectate: start (phase B, no death screen)` the same second as the death). The 2.5 s grace of v8.12 was a guess, so `PHASE_B_LIVE = false` (bossrules.rs): the game keeps its own death screen with the Resurrect choice, the reload after a real death is held (phase A) and the spectator camera follows during that wait. Re-enable only when the real charge count is found (RE: PlayerGameData resurrection count; goods 1100 Regenerative Power is the refill item, lot 2499). The v8.12 grace code and logs stay.

The friend's Resurrect was offered and used (14:05 log); the real co-op gap is the second one: Sekiro locks it
with `f32 PlayerGameData+0x150` (100 after rising, 0 after a respawn, cleared by SpEffects with
`revivalRecastDamage`), and in the logs only the player's OWN deathblow cleared it. `resurrect.rs` sends
`EV_REVIVE_UNLOCK` (event 44, relayed to all) when our deathblow starts (SpEffect 110030 + our throw running,
not in the first 5 s after a load, not right after a rest) or when the game clears our own lock while we live;

## v8.25a: sticky host ownership for bosses (ownfill)
`nsession::ownfill::Fill::step`: a host (slot 0) boss is no longer dropped when the game's own IsOwnedHere
answer turns nonzero (our forced bit and a nonzero native answer both return 1 through the detour, so the
drop only caused take/drop flip-flop and warping on the friend: ~25 pairs in 8 min for boss 1500800). The take
loop likewise ignores a nonzero native answer for a host boss. Remaining drops are unchanged (dead/unloaded,
`keep_boss` false for 5 s, conflict rules, reset). Pure rule `drop_on_native(me, is_boss)`. New `(boss)` log
lines (never rate limited): `ownfill: BOSS (boss) netId .. take|drop ..` with game_owned/forced/d_me/others/pos/hp/owners,
and `native owner answer a->b while forced (sticky: kept)` (at most once per 5 s per boss). Client path untouched.
## v8.25b: a peer's recent boss fight survives a load (spectate engaged)
`engaged` (bossrules.rs `engaged_ids`) needed `world.peer_boss(id)` or `our_fight && near`; the live `boss` is cleared on every
load/rest/death, so right after the host reloaded, a friend still in the same fight was not counted ("wait wanted false (engaged 0)").
world.rs `PeerWorld.last_boss` is set on `EV_BOSS(1)` and survives loads; it is cleared only by `EV_BOSS(2)` and `EV_DEATH`.
`peer_recent_boss(id, 15 s)` feeds `Engage.recent_boss`; `engaged_ids` = `(boss || (our_fight && (near || recent_boss))) && here && !down`.
Log: `death: peers [id boss near here down recent]`, `engage: peer N counted by recent-boss grace`, `engage: peer N not counted (...)`.
## v8.25c: stale pre-wipe state after a wipe
`persist::WIPE_AT` is set when a wipe is decided (`note_wipe`, the tick's `wipe_deaths`; not `note_wipe_loaded`).
For 90 s (`persist::WIPE_STALE`, `wipe_recent`) the friend's persist messages (frozen()), livesync lives
(`take_lives` dropped, remote/baseline/sent cleared on the rising edge), puppet's BULK_DEAD_LIST kill_ids,
`force_dead_one` (no kill, no loot) and posture's lives mirror are ignored. posture.rs line ~384 (own change
counting) is untouched: it is about our own drops, not the peer's. Known weakness: a friend still in the old
world for more than 90 s gets through; a real fix needs a wipe epoch stamped on packets (protocol bump, out of scope).

## v8.26d: F11 dump shows scaling and posture (diagnostics only)
F11 now also logs `scale: F11 ...` lines (scaling.rs `snapshot_lines`, called from statelog.rs `dump`): the active ScaleCfg, player count and boss list (a read-only mirror `MIRROR` that `Scaling::apply` fills), then up to 12 living enemies nearest the player with NpcParam hp/posture, the base max hp/posture now in the data module (+0x138/+0x150), the wanted scaled values, `ours` (would our write be accepted), current hp, current posture (+0x148) and max posture (+0x14C), plus net id and `owned_here`. If `base` posture equals `npc` posture while `want` is larger and `ours` is true, our write is not landing; if `ours` is false something else changed it; if base == want but `max_posture` stays at the NpcParam value, the game does not recompute max posture from the base. The player's own lock-on target has no accessor: the LOCKED line uses the spectate lock mark (`spectate_cam::lock_mark`) and the nearest enemy within 6 m of it, so it only appears while spectating.
## v8.26e: sen / emblems around the mod's own death reload
The mod's null-argument death reload (0xA8F7E0, `bossrules::mod_death_reload`: spectator released on a wipe, and the
`replay_or_reload` fallback) leaves carried Spirit Emblems and sen at 0 (live: 15 emblems + 303 sen -> 0). A vanilla
death (Lua warp -> 0x6D7070) keeps emblems and halves sen. The proper fix (replaying the held load request) is unsafe
(stale stack pointer args), so invguard snapshots emblems/sen/xp before the call and, once the load is done (>= 3 s,
snapshot < 180 s old), gives back exactly the snapshot emblems and sen/2, only when the value is exactly 0, once.
Ini `[GAMEPLAY] restore_lost_items = 1` (0 = log only). Phase B now also logs the held load args for the proper fix.
## 58. Resurrect input dump (v8.20, 2026-10-25, source only: not built, not tested, NOT verified live)

Read-only logging for the open Resurrect question (NOTES §56 H1/H2). `invguard::resurrect_fields()` reads `PlayerGameData + 0x150` (f32 "Revive Recast Point", the lock) and the amounts of goods 1110 / 1100 / 2000 (`audit::read_inventory`, `-` = not in the inventory). `statelog::resurrect_dump(why)` logs that plus every SpEffect on the player as `resurrect dump (<why>): ...; SpEffects [..]`. Called: first dead frame (bossrules.rs, next to `death: first dead frame`), on `resurrect: OFFERED / NOT offered`, on F11, and whenever the lock value changes (invguard `watch_revive_lock`, 500 ms resolution, 200 lines). Test (two PCs): the friend dies in a boss fight, then kills one enemy the host owns and one of their own; compare the lock and SpEffect 400 across the lines on both machines. Reading goods 2000 from the inventory is a guess (it may be a flag, not an item): `-` there is not proof. Written in a Linux cloud session without the MSVC toolchain: run `F:\Temp\cj.cmd` first.

## v8.27a: better logs (session header, wall-clock times, event timeline)
Behaviour unchanged; every line still starts with `[epoch] `. At DLL start log.rs `session_header()` writes `==== sekiro-coop session start ====`, `version: sekiro_coop v<crate version> build <git hash> (built epoch E = local HH:MM:SS)` (new crates/coop-dll/build.rs embeds `COOP_GIT_HASH` and `COOP_BUILD_EPOCH` via `cargo:rustc-env`, std only, "unknown" without git), `clock: epoch E = local HH:MM:SS (UTC offset +hh:mm)` and `EVENT SESSION_START build=<hash>`. The `clock:` line repeats every 600 s (checked inside `log::line`) and before every `MARK F11 (state dump) at HH:MM:SS local`. The UTC offset comes from kernel32 `GetTimeZoneInformation` (DST aware). coop-audit's `parse` only needs the `[digits]` prefix; none of the new lines match its line prefixes (`CRASH`, `testsync: host has build`, `net:`, `session:` ...).
`log::event(kind, detail)` writes `EVENT <kind> <detail>`, never rate limited. Planned kinds, to be adopted by other modules: BOSS_START, BOSS_END, DEATH, WIPE, REST, LOAD, RELEASE.

## v8.28a: spectate release replays the held death-warp request (NOT verified live)
Item loss evidence: two save backups show the Sculptor's storage emblems 130 -> 0 (2026-10-25) and 45 -> 0 (2026-10-26), each right after a spectate release that called 0xA8F7E0 with null args (`mod_death_reload`); Resurrect nodes (goods 1100) and some consumables vanished the same way. A vanilla death never calls 0xA8F7E0: Lua event warp -> load request 0x6D7070 (caller RVA 0x68e859), which the detour holds during spectate. The phase-B release now replays that held request (`replay_held_request("phase B")`: `call_original(&LOAD_REQ_TRAMP, a0..a3)` from LOAD_HELD_ARGS, state RELEASING so the detour lets it through; invguard snapshot still taken). Decision helper `phase_b_release(held, flag)` -> ReplayHeld / NullReload / LetDeathStep. Ini `[GAMEPLAY] spectate_replay_load = 1` (default); 0 = old null-arg path (log: `spectate_replay_load = 0: using the old null-arg death reload`). The no-held-request else-branch of `replay_or_reload` (phase A / force_reload: the game issued no load) is unchanged and still calls 0xA8F7E0: nothing was held and it is not known that the game's death step would run there.
Test live: spectate through a wipe, then check emblems + storage + Resurrect nodes survive; log line `replayed the held load request (phase B)`. Unknown: the args are assumed to be plain values (b = 0x0f000000 looks like a map id). If the player stays dead after the replay, the RELEASING watchdog forces the old reload later.

## v8.29a: a host-owned boss is not ForceDead while its deathblow throw still plays (NOT verified live)
Evidence (Corrupted Monk 1500800, host): the friend's final deathblow plays ~8 s on the host; the copy looked frozen, then livesync's Plan::Kill ran `force_dead_one` (EMEVD ForceDead) with HP > 0 and the boss ended with no death animation. Now, for a tracked single-life boss (`tracked && !multi`), Plan::Kill is deferred while `enemies::throw_active(chr)` and under `BOSS_THROW_WAIT` (12 s); `boss_wait` (netId -> first deferred, line logged) in the livesync State; counted in stats `held`; remote lives/dead bookkeeping untouched so the plan re-runs every scan. When the throw ends or the cap hits, the unchanged kill runs (loot/persist logic untouched, no duplicate loot). If the copy dies by itself the entry is dropped. Log lines: `boss netId ..: ... ForceDead waits`, `throw over / 12 s cap after N ms: ForceDead`, `died by itself after N ms (no ForceDead)`. Pure helper `boss_kill_waits(throw_active, waited)`.
## v8.29b: keep the friend's pin released while the remote is still in a throw (NOT verified live)
A boss deathblow by the friend lasts ~8 s on the host, but `nsession::note_throw` opens a 4 s window only (`REMOTE_THROW_MS`); the pin re-engaged mid-throw and pulled the remote out of the paired animation. `native.rs follow_peers` now releases the pin while `remote_in_throw(slot) || enemies::throw_active(r.chr)`, helper `pin_released(in_window, node_throw, since)`, hard cap 15 s (`PIN_THROW_CAP`) per throw. Logs once per throw: `native: peer N's remote still in a throw at X ms: pin kept off`; at the cap `native: pin kept off for 15 s: giving up, pin back on`. Behaviour without a throw is unchanged.

`[DEBUG] f11_pad_button = back|start|l3|r3|none` (default back, XInput 0x0020; `tuning::F11_PAD_MASK`). `puppet.rs on_frame` ORs a rising edge of that button (`spectate_input::pad_buttons_down()` = first connected pad of 0..3, 1 s retry backoff; helper `pad_edge`) into `f11_down`, so the MARK line and the state dump fire as with the keyboard F11. ## v8.30b: the host yields a non-boss enemy at once when the friend owns it (NOT verified live)
Evidence: ~56 % of nearby enemies were owned on both machines (host forced bit + friend game-native ownership; `claimed by both machines` 1-7 per 10 s), so both ran their own AI and desynced. Changes (nsession.rs): `ownfill::conflict_drop(me, owners, for, is_boss)` returns true at once for the host when `!is_boss && !owners.is_empty()` (client and boss rules unchanged; host bosses stay sticky). `Fill.cooldown` (netId -> drop time) is set on every "another player owns it" drop and blocks a retake for `RETAKE_COOLDOWN` = 10 s; `reset()` clears it and the `logs` counter. Those "given back" lines bypass the 30-line cap, one per netId per 5 s. ## v8.31a: 'Mod settings' panel next to the game's System menu

What it is: while the game's pause-menu System list (the one holding Quit Game) is open, an ImGui window 'Mod settings' sits at the right edge. The host (or a player with no session) edits enemy HP %, enemy posture %, boss HP %, boss posture % (0..1000) and the death debuff; Apply stores the atomics, writes the ini (`settings::write_keys`, tmp file + rename) and the existing ScaleCfg resend (puppet.rs `scale_sent`) sends it to the friends. A client sees the host's values read-only plus 'Set by the host'. Key `ingame_menu = 0` turns everything off (no panel, no message filter, stamp ignored). `ingame_menu_key` (VK, default 45 Insert) collapses / expands.

Parts: `quitblock.rs pred_detour` stamps the time when the predicate is asked about the Quit command (return value unchanged); `puppet.rs menu_frame` runs the latch (`menu::latch_step`, pure) and applies edits; `menu.rs` holds the shared state; `ui.rs Panel` draws it and `message_filter` returns `InputMouse` only while the panel is visible and `want_capture_mouse`. The host's `death_debuffs` is now authoritative: ScaleCfg got a 10th byte (`debuffs`); old 9-byte bodies decode to `None` (the receiver keeps its own), old decoders ignore the byte. `doctor.rs` still logs 'death_debuffs differs (each side uses its own)'; that line is now outdated for clients (not touched, outside this change's files).

NOT verified live (no game run):
- How often the game calls the predicate for Quit while the System list is idle. The latch is therefore never closed by a stale stamp alone, only by menu mask 0, a pending load, not in world, dead, or the 10 min limit. Side effect: after leaving the System list for another pause-menu page the panel may stay up until the whole menu closes.
- Whether the menu mask is non-zero while the System list is open (the latch needs `menu_mask() != Some(0)` to open).
- Whether the game shows a mouse cursor in menus. `io.mouse_draw_cursor` is set while the panel is visible; if the game has its own cursor there will be two.
- Whether mouse clicks reach the game's menu through the panel (the `InputMouse` filter should stop them) and whether the game's menu reacts to a mouse at all.
- Whether the game binds Insert (keyboard is never filtered).
- The live mid-session resend of the changed ScaleCfg to the friend (the code path is the existing one; check the host log for `scale: sent` and the client log for `scale: host uses` after Apply).
- hudhook 0.9.3 `message_filter` is evaluated once per frame from the previous frame's `want_capture_mouse`.

Not attempted: a native entry in the game's own System list (needs IDA work on the menu list builder).
## v8.31b: a death next to a multi-life enemy starts the spectate wait even if it was not hurt (NOT verified live)
Evidence: host log 2026-10-26 06:48, two deaths to a Headless with the friend near: `our fight false`, no `boss: miniboss fight` line (never scratched / friend >25 m from it / maybe lives0 = 1). bossrules.rs: `mini_scan` caches the nearest living multi-life enemy within MINI_NEAR_M (`mini_near`, any engagement) and our last scan position (`last_me`); at the first dead frame a cache younger than 3 s makes `our_fight` true via the pure `our_fight_at_death(..)` (log `death: died within N m of multi-life enemy ...`). When our_fight is still false the first dead frame logs `death: nearby enemies [entity lives0 hp/max dist]` (up to 5, lives0 >= 1, within 40 m). `mini_engaged`, gauge path and wipe/release logic unchanged.
Risk: a death next to a sleeping multi-life enemy (e.g. fall damage) now starts a spectate wait; it ends by the existing rules (friend dies, rests, leaves, wait timers). If Headless has lives0 = 1 this does not help; the nearby-enemies line will show it.

## v8.32a: controller control for the Mod settings panel (NOT verified live)
Ini `[GAMEPLAY] ingame_menu_pad = 1` (default on; 0 = mouse/keyboard only). Host only, panel visible and not collapsed. `spectate_input::pad_state()` (XInput, first pad 0..3, 1 s backoff after a miss) is polled from the render thread in `ui.rs Panel::draw` and fed to the pure `menu::PadNav` (unit-tested). Inputs: right stick Y beyond +/-18000 = row up/down (edge, 300 ms repeat, wraps); right stick X = value +/-5 (first push, then after 350 ms every 90 ms), +/-25 with LB or RB held; RT >= 200 = Apply (edge, re-arms below 80, starts disarmed so a held trigger does nothing); R3 edge = flip the death debuff when that row is selected. Selected row is marked with a yellow '>'. A client sees no pad hint and no pad control.
Deliberately NOT used: D-pad, A, B, X, Y, Start, Back (Back = F11 mark). The game's pause menu reads D-pad/A/B and we do not hook XInput (Themida-packed game; its input API is unknown).
UNVERIFIED in the game:
- Whether the System menu reacts to the right stick / triggers / R3 / LB / RB. If it does, set `ingame_menu_pad = 0` or use the mouse.
- Whether a PS4/PS5 pad appears as XInput under Steam Input (if not, `pad_state()` returns None and nothing happens).
- Whether XInputGetState from the render thread is fine in practice (it is a plain read; no game memory is touched).

## v8.33: the emblem/sen restore guard watches 45 s (friend's live log 2026-10-26)
v8.26e's guard sampled once 3 s after the mod's null-arg reload and cleared its snapshot (`mod death reload kept emblems/sen (11/5)`); the wipe (`emblems carried 11 -> 0 (sen 0)`) landed 2 s later, after the loading screen. Now `invguard::restore_after_reload` keeps the snapshot for `SNAP_WINDOW` = 45 s, samples every 500 ms outside loading screens, and restores only a loss (emblems or sen exactly 0 while the snapshot was > 0) that stays visible for `ZERO_HOLD` = 2 s (`verdict`, pure, unit-tested). Log: `invguard: restored after mod death reload ...` or `... nothing lost within 45 s ..., guard off`. NOT verified live; the v8.28 held-request replay normally avoids the reload altogether, this guard is the fallback.

## v8.34b: pure row model for the native Co-op Settings page (optmenu_model.rs)

## v8.35a: FFI primitives for the native Co-op Settings page (msfunc.rs, nativetext.rs)
- `msfunc.rs`: MSVC `std::function` objects in Rust (`FnImpl {vtbl, row_id}` inline at storage[0..16], impl pointer at +56, 3 static vtables getter/setter/list, all bodies in `catch_unwind`, `FAILED` latch). `set_handlers(get, set, list)` registers the callbacks, `init_function(storage, kind, row_id)` builds one. The setter takes `*const u8` (MSVC passes the forwarded argument by reference).
- `nativetext.rs`: ids `0x3B9A_0000..+0x1_0000` -> leaked UTF-16 (`intern`), relabels of 110700/110605 (only while `[GAMEPLAY] native_mod_menu = 1`), one-shot `arm_override(label, help)` for 110710/110701. `quitblock::msg_detour` calls `nativetext::lookup(bank, id, orig)` after the original and returns the pointer (a `const wchar_t*`, same kind as the original result).
- `tuning::NATIVE_MOD_MENU` + ini key `native_mod_menu` (default 0). Nothing is hooked yet; the page builder comes with optmenu.rs.

## v8.35c: native Co-op Settings page replaces the Network Settings page builder (kind 2), NOT verified live
`optmenu.rs`, ini `[GAMEPLAY] native_mod_menu = 1` (default 0). Detours the kind-2 page builder `0x140CCDED0(int a1, i64 a2)` (7 bytes `40 57 B8 20 10 00 00`) always; with the flag off, the mod not alive or `msfunc::FAILED` latched it calls the trampoline. All 13 natives are prologue-checked against bytes read from the IDA db (F:\Temp\ida_opt\o15); one mismatch = `optmenu: OFF (<fn> prologue unverified)` and nothing installed.
- Sequence: `0x1419DE730(4696, 8, heap)` (thunk to `heap->vtbl[10]`, `heap = *0x143D9CE28`), ctor `0x140CB8980(page, a1, a2)`, then per model row: `0x140CC5D10(label176)` with `nativetext::arm_override(label, help)`, `0x140CADBA0(page, label, &11, &{7,0,L"02_201_CommandList"}, &get, &set, &list, &empty)`, `0x140C609A0(label)`; finally `0x140CA0AB0(page, 0)`. No native row (`a2+109` untouched), no Defaults row.
- Deviation from the addendum: `0x1419DE750` is the DEALLOCATION (IDA: `test rcx,rcx; jz; ... free`), reached by the original only in its constructor-exception path. It is never called.
- Lists: `build_items` follows `0x140CFDA60` per item (text object via `0x140C7B910(tmp, id)`, copy `0x1401C7130(item+16, tmp+8)`, `0x1401C8720(item+72, L"", heap)`, append `0x140CFF3C0`, `0x140CBBC30(item)`, inline free of the text object). On/Off labels use the game's own ids 40400/40401, everything else `nativetext::intern`. Items carry value = model index.
- Values: five scaling rows from the tuning atomics (client: host's ScaleCfg values), others from the ini (cached 1 s, defaults from the built-in template via `settings::default_ini_text()`), plus a 5 s pending-edit map. Sets: scaling rows via `menu::post_edit` only while `ingame_menu = 1` (puppet.rs drops edits otherwise), all other rows (and scaling rows when the panel is off) via `settings::write_keys`. Client / locked rows are refused (`optmenu: client tried host row <key>: refused`).
- Logs: `optmenu: prologue <fn> = ...`, `optmenu: 13/13 prologues ok, page builder detoured`, `optmenu: Network Settings page replaced: N rows`, `optmenu: set <key> = <v> (role R)`.

## v8.35: Co-op Settings page review fixes (Opus review of optmenu.rs, 2026-10-26)
Fixed before shipping: (1) CRASH: the text object of `0x140C7B910` is 56 bytes (flag byte at +48), the Rust buffer was 48 (stack overwrite by one byte): now 64; (2) CRASH on failure: the game's display lambda dereferences item 0 of the list, so `h_list` never returns an empty list now; (3) optmenu stays OFF without the text-lookup detour (`quitblock::msg_hooked`); (4) the heap for item wstrings is read fresh per call. Default `native_mod_menu = 0`; enable with `native_mod_menu = 1`. Live checks: 13/13 prologues ok line + `quit: 2/2 grey-out hooks`; open/close the page 3 times; change an On/Off, a Choice and a Pct row (ini gets the value, the row shows it immediately); off-grid ini value (boss_health_scaling = 65) shows `+65 %`; client rows are read-only; FPS with the page open; the setter wrapper CC09E0 passes the item's +0 value byte.

Live 2026-10-27 Divine Dragon (host log 16:20:44-16:24:53): the host died (second death, Resurrect locked) and spectated; the dragon 2500800 stayed forced on the host (`owned_here=true forced=true`, the game's own answer flipped 1->0). The friend brought its HP to 1 at 16:22:07 and it stayed there 165 s: m25 event 12505880 waits for `HP ratio <= 0` (immortal until the player's deathblow), then HBD + 9310. The deathblow never came on the machine that ran the dragon (its player was dead).

## v8.60: the host's type 5 does not kill a Puppeteer puppet on the client (2026-10-28, NOT verified live)
v8.59 kept livesync / persist off a puppet (`statelog::owner_team_state`: SpEffect stateInfo 302 active and chr+0x70 == 2). The remaining kill was the enemy owner's HP table. IDA (`F:\Temp\re_puppet2`, o1/o3, copy of the re_puppet db) [VAR]:
- `0xA4B830` client branch (native session, `0xA4BA81` IsClient): for each chr with an entry (`0xA1C7A0`, sentinel `0xF987`): `hp_before = 0x5A9C80(data)` (r15d), `0xBD64E0(data, entry.hp)` (call `0xA4BB88`, returns to `0xA4BB8D`), `0x9F6950(chr, entry.flags)`, then `if hp_before > 0 && 0xBD5D40(data)` -> `0x9EE7E0(chr, 0)`. The kill is **remote = 0** (the brief and RE-FINDINGS-2 said 1: that is NetworkManipulator `0x7EC250`, remote players only, and the peer damage path `0xA4F940`). Callers of `0x9EE7E0`: `0x74FAA0`, `0x7EC43C` (dl = r13b), `0x9F9D42`, `0xA4BBB7`, `0xA4FC16` (dl = 1), `0xB97812`.
- `0x9EE7E0` itself first writes HP 0 and the type-5 setter has already written 0 before it runs, so a kill-only block would leave a 0-HP puppet. Chosen instead: detour the HP setter `0xBD64E0` (prologue `48 89 5C 24 18 89 54 24 10 57 48 83 EC 20`, 14 bytes, nothing RIP-relative) and skip only the call that returns to `0xA4BB8D` when the chr is a puppet here. HP stays, `0xBD5D40` (HP <= 0 and no SpEffect hold) stays false, the kill is not reached. Any type-5 HP (also > 0) is skipped for a puppet: its HP is local, the owner's copy is a corpse.
- chr from the data module: `data+0x8` (`0xB271D0` returns it; `0xBD5D40` reads `chr+0x11D0` = our CHR_SP_LIST through it) [VAR].
- The caller: `hook::install_detour_caller_r8`, a variant of `install_detour` whose stub also does `mov r8, r11` (the return address), so a 2-argument detour gets it as its 3rd argument. `detour_caller` is one slot shared by every thread, and the setter is called from many places (30 callers, chr updates may run on worker threads).
- Cost: every HP write in the game goes through the stub + one compare; the SpEffect walk runs only for the type-5 caller.
- Not guarded [I]: `0x9F6950(chr, entry.flags)` still applies the host's flags (bit 0 -> data module+0x20 bit 1, else an animation-ish call through vtbl+0x568); unknown effect on a puppet. The peer damage path `0xA4F940` (HP delta + kill remote = 1) is not touched: the peer's copy is a corpse and cannot hit.
- Mode 4 (`enemies.rs client_frame`, no native session) had the same hole (`chr.set_hp(s.hp)` with the host's 0, NetAI / position from the host's corpse): `puppetguard::mode4_hold` treats a puppet like a rest-held enemy, checked only when the host says dead and our copy is alive.
- Symmetric case (host's puppet): the host never applies type 5 (its `0xA4B830` branch only fills the table), livesync / persist are covered by v8.59. Open [I]: in the native session the puppet's AI owner is still decided by the type-22 split; if the host owns the client's puppet (or the reverse), NetAI may drive it from the other machine's corpse.
- Logs: `type5: enemy E netId N is a puppet here: host's kill (HP 0) ignored, HP kept at H (k type-5 writes skipped so far)` (once per chr); mode 4: `enemies: enemy E is a puppet here (HP h), dead on the host: host's HP 0 and AI ignored`. Kill switch `[GAMEPLAY] puppet_type5_guard = 0` (both).

## v8.61: stale PGD+0x178 after phase B, lazy puppet walk, patch-freeze backoff (2026-10-28, NOT verified live)
- Evidence (logs 2026-10-28 10:05-11:02, v8.59): `resurrect dump ... PGD+0x178` at the first dead frame was 1 in all 5 deaths that had lock 0 + nodes and were NOT offered, 0 in all 17 OFFERED ones. Each of the 5 followed a phase B death of the same player. The game sets the byte when Resurrect is not taken and clears it during its own death screen within ~10 s (host 10:40:13 -> 10:40:23); phase B creates no CSDeadStep, so the byte survived the reload and `0xA2CC30` kept the next window shut.
- `bossrules.rs`: `resurrect_eligible(.., no_revive)` (byte set = not eligible -> phase B, no black death screen); `stale_no_revive_step`: once alive (HP > 0) after a phase B death, the byte is written back to 0 (`clear_stale_no_revive`, `[GAMEPLAY] clear_stale_no_revive`). Writing restores the vanilla post-death state [I: the other 30 writers of a +0x178 byte (IDA re_rev2 o2) were not checked for PlayerGameData]. `watch_no_revive`: every change logged (250 ms poll).
- Frame cost: v8.59 `statelog::owner_team_state` (SpEffect walk, 4 ReadProcessMemory per node) ran for every enemy on every livesync / persist scan; gameplay mod avg 0.73 -> 1.55 / 1.80 ms, ticks with a mod frame > 10 ms 3% -> 58% / 85%. `livesync::puppet_check_needed(hp, dead_before, remote_dead)`: the puppet flag can only change the dead test (HP 0), the corpse rule (dead before) and a Kill (friend's copy dead), so the walk is skipped otherwise; persist the same (HP 0 or in the dead set).
- Patch freezes (host only): `write_all_timed`'s budget is checked between threads, so one blocking GetThreadContext (nvwgf2umx.dll thread, 5378 ms) overran it, and nsession retried a timed-out engage every frame (12 tries in 7 s). Now `busy_retry_gap` 1/2/4/8 s after each timed-out try (`Busy(0)` = timeout, own message), 12 in a row = give up; `freeze::SKIP_DRIVER_THREADS` leaves threads started in GPU driver DLLs (`is_driver_module`) unsuspended (they never execute sekiro.exe patch sites). Open: every ENGAGE / DISENGAGE still freezes 105 threads for 100-300 ms on the host.
- Profile: `crash::breadcrumb` charges the time to the next breadcrumb (thread-local, only inside `prof_frame_start/end` of the frame task); new breadcrumbs restsync / livesync / persist / audit / death sync / scaling / after net frame. The tick line names the top 3 spans when a mod frame exceeded 5 ms. `net: link:` status line (ping) every 30 s.
